# Introduction | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/readme.md)
.

Breeze uses JavaScript for scripting. If you aren't familiar with JavaScript, it's suggested that you find a tutorial to teach you the basics.

Create a new script by creating a file ending in `.js` in the `breeze/1.8.9/scripting` directory.

We highly recommend you get our [VSCode Extension](https://scripting.breeze.rip/vscode_extension)
 extension, it makes scripting with breeze a breeze.

Next we recommend you look at [Getting Started](https://scripting.breeze.rip/getting_started)
 or some of the [Examples](https://scripting.breeze.rip/examples/pingspoof)
.

Last updated 24 days ago

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/vscode_extension.md)
.

For writing Breeze scripts we recommend using [Visual Studio Code.](https://code.visualstudio.com/)
 Our dedicated VS Code extension provides full IntelliSense for the entire Breeze API, including automatic type inference and inline documentation. You can install it directly from the Visual Studio Code Marketplace.

# Defining Settings | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings.md)
.

Settings allow users to configure your script's behavior directly from the client UI. All settings must be created during script initialization.

### Setting Types[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#setting-types)

#### BooleanSetting[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#booleansetting)

A simple toggle (on/off).

Copy

    const enabled = new BooleanSetting(script, "Enabled", "Whether the feature is active.", true);
    
    // Reading the value
    if (enabled.getValue()) {
        // do something
    }

#### IntSetting[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#intsetting)

An integer value with a defined range.

Copy

    const delay = new IntSetting(script, "Delay", "Ticks between actions.", 5, 0, 20);
    
    // With a slider step
    const speed = new IntSetting(script, "Speed", "Movement speed multiplier.", 10, 0, 100, 5);
    
    var value = delay.getValue(); // number

#### DoubleSetting[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#doublesetting)

A decimal value with a defined range.

#### ModeSetting[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#modesetting)

A dropdown that lets the user pick from a list of named modes.

#### RangeSetting[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#rangesetting)

A dual-handle slider representing a min/max range.

### Subgroups[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#subgroups)

Related settings can be organized under a `SubGroup` to keep the UI tidy.

> Pass the `SubGroup` instance as the first argument instead of `script`.

### Conditional Visibility[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#conditional-visibility)

You can hide settings dynamically based on the state of other settings using `.visible()`.

Last updated 3 months ago

*   [Setting Types](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#setting-types)
    
*   [Subgroups](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#subgroups)
    
*   [Conditional Visibility](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings#conditional-visibility)
    

Copy

    const reach = new DoubleSetting(script, "Reach", "Attack reach in blocks.", 3.0, 1.0, 6.0);
    
    // With a slider step
    const factor = new DoubleSetting(script, "Factor", "Boost factor.", 1.5, 0.5, 5.0, 0.5);
    
    var value = reach.getValue(); // number

Copy

    const mode = new ModeSetting(script, "Mode", "Which algorithm to use.", "Fast", ["Fast", "Silent", "Legit"]);
    
    if (mode.is("Fast")) {
        // fast path
    }
    
    var current = mode.getValue(); // "Fast" | "Silent" | "Legit"

Copy

    const cps = new RangeSetting(script, "CPS", "Clicks per second range.", 8, 12, 1, 20, 1);
    
    var min = cps.getMin();   // number
    var max = cps.getMax();   // number
    var val = cps.random();   // random value within the selected range

Copy

    const rotations = new SubGroup("Rotations");
    
    const smoothing = new BooleanSetting(rotations, "Smooth", "Smoothly rotate to targets.", true);
    const rotSpeed  = new DoubleSetting(rotations, "Speed", "Max degrees per tick.", 8.0, 1.0, 180.0);

Copy

    const smooth = new BooleanSetting(script, "Smooth", "Enable smooth rotations.", true);
    const speed  = new DoubleSetting(script, "Speed", "Rotation speed.", 8.0, 1.0, 180.0);
    
    // Only show 'speed' when 'smooth' is enabled
    speed.visible(() => smooth.getValue());

# Getting Started | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/getting_started.md)
.

#### Creating the script file.[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/getting_started#creating-the-script-file)

To start you have to create a javascript file inside of the breeze scripting folder. You can find this folder in two ways:

*   Pressing the icon inside of the breeze scripting tab. ![](https://scripting.breeze.rip/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3854170834-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FiOeZYpMqko1q0SgE11To%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-060dba2f11ca9918eccc5e204de5c22e16a926ae%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c305870a&sv=2)
    
*   Or manually manouver to the appdata folder, the same folder where [`.minecraft`](https://stickypiston.co/account/knowledgebase/130/How-do-I-find-my-Minecraft-Folder.html)
     is. In the folder navigate the following path to find the breeze scripting folder -> `breeze/1.8.9/scripts`
    

In the `scripts` directory create a file with the name of your script ending with `.js`.

The script name that shows up inside of breeze is defined by what you name the file! For example calling your file `PingSpoof.js` will make the script show up as **PingSpoof**.\\

![](https://scripting.breeze.rip/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F3854170834-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FiOeZYpMqko1q0SgE11To%252Fuploads%252Fgit-blob-b15ef44fcd4bee85e034a9e88775d57993e70e5e%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia&width=300&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=7aec4e96&sv=2)

#### Script Description[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/getting_started#script-description)

To start your script we can use the [script](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/script)
 to set the description for the script.

Copy

    script.description = "Test description for this script";

Last updated 8 months ago

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/getting\_started.md). # Getting Started #### Creating the script file. To start you have to create a javascript file inside of the breeze scripting folder. You can find this folder in two ways: \* Pressing the _:folder:_ icon inside of the breeze scripting tab.\\ !\[\](/files/ZaTIooPMcud6YtbYQho4) \* Or manually manouver to the appdata folder, the same folder where \[\`.minecraft\`\](https://stickypiston.co/account/knowledgebase/130/How-do-I-find-my-Minecraft-Folder.html) is. In the folder navigate the following path to find the breeze scripting folder -> \`breeze/1.8.9/scripts\` In the \`scripts\` directory create a file with the name of your script ending with \`.js\`. {% hint style="success" %} The script name that shows up inside of breeze is defined by what you name the file! For example calling your file \`PingSpoof.js\` will make the script show up as \*\*PingSpoof\*\*.\\\\ ![](https://scripting.breeze.rip/files/I80yVbgg0irDCsnmIV0S) {% endhint %} #### Script Description To start your script we can use the \[script\](/api/namespaces/script.md) to set the description for the script. \`\`\`javascript script.description = "Test description for this script"; \`\`\`

# Event Listeners | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/event_listeners.md)
.

Use `script.addListener` to react to game events. All listeners must be registered during script initialization.

#### Basic Usage[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/event_listeners#basic-usage)

Copy

    script.addListener("EventName", (event) => {
        // handle event
    });

[https://github.com/LoseBypass/breeze-docs/blob/main/docs/api/events/README.md](https://github.com/LoseBypass/breeze-docs/blob/main/docs/api/events/README.md)

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining\_settings.md).

# Defining Settings

Settings allow users to configure your script's behavior directly from the client UI. All settings must be created during script initialization.

### Setting Types

#### BooleanSetting

A simple toggle (on/off).

\`\`\`js
const enabled = new BooleanSetting(script, "Enabled", "Whether the feature is active.", true);

// Reading the value
if (enabled.getValue()) {
    // do something
}
\`\`\`

#### IntSetting

An integer value with a defined range.

\`\`\`js
const delay = new IntSetting(script, "Delay", "Ticks between actions.", 5, 0, 20);

// With a slider step
const speed = new IntSetting(script, "Speed", "Movement speed multiplier.", 10, 0, 100, 5);

var value = delay.getValue(); // number
\`\`\`

#### DoubleSetting

A decimal value with a defined range.

\`\`\`javascript
const reach = new DoubleSetting(script, "Reach", "Attack reach in blocks.", 3.0, 1.0, 6.0);

// With a slider step
const factor = new DoubleSetting(script, "Factor", "Boost factor.", 1.5, 0.5, 5.0, 0.5);

var value = reach.getValue(); // number
\`\`\`

#### ModeSetting

A dropdown that lets the user pick from a list of named modes.

\`\`\`js
const mode = new ModeSetting(script, "Mode", "Which algorithm to use.", "Fast", \["Fast", "Silent", "Legit"\]);

if (mode.is("Fast")) {
    // fast path
}

var current = mode.getValue(); // "Fast" | "Silent" | "Legit"
\`\`\`

#### RangeSetting

A dual-handle slider representing a min/max range.

\`\`\`js
const cps = new RangeSetting(script, "CPS", "Clicks per second range.", 8, 12, 1, 20, 1);

var min = cps.getMin();   // number
var max = cps.getMax();   // number
var val = cps.random();   // random value within the selected range
\`\`\`

### Subgroups

Related settings can be organized under a \`SubGroup\` to keep the UI tidy.

\`\`\`js
const rotations = new SubGroup("Rotations");

const smoothing = new BooleanSetting(rotations, "Smooth", "Smoothly rotate to targets.", true);
const rotSpeed  = new DoubleSetting(rotations, "Speed", "Max degrees per tick.", 8.0, 1.0, 180.0);
\`\`\`

> Pass the \`SubGroup\` instance as the first argument instead of \`script\`.

### Conditional Visibility

You can hide settings dynamically based on the state of other settings using \`.visible()\`.

\`\`\`js
const smooth = new BooleanSetting(script, "Smooth", "Enable smooth rotations.", true);
const speed  = new DoubleSetting(script, "Speed", "Rotation speed.", 8.0, 1.0, 180.0);

// Only show 'speed' when 'smooth' is enabled
speed.visible(() => smooth.getValue());
\`\`\`

# Rotation Manager | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager.md)
.

The `rotationManager` namespace lets your script control the player's look direction (yaw/pitch).

* * *

### Setup[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#setup)

Set your priority during initialization. Higher numbers run first.

Copy

    rotationManager.setPriority(10);

### Registering Callbacks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#registering-callbacks)

#### **onRotate**[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#onrotate)

This is your main rotation callback — called every tick when your script has priority. Call `rotationManager.rotate()` inside here.

Copy

    rotationManager.onRotate(() => {
        rotationManager.rotate(targetYaw, targetPitch, 8.0);
    });

#### onUpdateNoRotate[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#onupdatenorotate)

Called every tick when another module has taken priority over yours. Use this to keep internal state updated even when you're not rotating.

Copy

    rotationManager.onUpdateNoRotate(() => {
        // another module is rotating — idle tick
    });

### Rotating[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#rotating)

#### [`rotationManager.rotate(yaw, pitch, speed)`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/rotationmanager.rotate)
[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#rotationmanager.rotate-yaw-pitch-speed)

Smoothly steps toward the target yaw/pitch by at most `speed` degrees per tick. Returns `true` when the target has been reached.

### Reading the Current Spoofed Angles[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#reading-the-current-spoofed-angles)

You can read back the current spoofed rotation at any time — useful for calculating aim vectors or checking if you're on target.

### Priority List[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#priority-list)

These are the default priorities for each module in breeze. Highest priority gets to rotate first.

Module

Priority

SelfTrap

45

BedNuker

40

AntiFireball

35

Scaffold

30

Clutch

25

MLG

20

KnockBackManipulator

15

SilentAim

10

AutoRod

5

KillAura

0

Last updated 3 months ago

*   [Setup](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#setup)
    
*   [Registering Callbacks](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#registering-callbacks)
    
*   [Rotating](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#rotating)
    
*   [Reading the Current Spoofed Angles](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#reading-the-current-spoofed-angles)
    
*   [Priority List](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation_manager#priority-list)
    

Copy

    rotationManager.onRotate(() => {
        const done = rotationManager.rotate(90.0, 15.0, 6.0);
    
        if (done) {
            // we're now looking exactly at the target
        }
    });

Copy

    var yaw   = rotationManager.getSpoofedYaw();
    var pitch = rotationManager.getSpoofedPitch();

# Spoof Manager | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager.md)
.

The `spoofManager` namespace lets your script intercept, cancel, delay, and release packets in both directions. Like the rotation manager, it uses a priority system for when multiple scripts are active.

This manager is mainly meant for making lag based modules. For basic packet manipulation we recommend [PacketSendEvent](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/packetsendevent)
.

* * *

### Setup[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#setup)

Set your priority during initialization.

Copy

    spoofManager.setPriority(10);

* * *

### Intercepting Packets[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#intercepting-packets)

#### `onPacketSend`[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#onpacketsend)

Called for every outbound packet (client → server). Return a `SpoofAction` to decide what happens to it.

Copy

    spoofManager.onPacketSend((packet) => {
        if (packet instanceof C0BPacketEntityAction) {
            if (packet.getAction() === "START_SNEAKING") {
                return "CANCEL"; // block sneak packets from reaching the server
            }
        }
        return "PROCESS"; // let everything else through
    });

* * *

#### `onPacketReceive`[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#onpacketreceive)

Called for every inbound packet (server → client). Same `SpoofAction` return values apply.

* * *

### SpoofAction Values[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#spoofaction-values)

Value

Description

`"PROCESS"`

Do nothing, let the packet continue as normal

`"CANCEL"`

Drop the packet entirely — it will never be processed

`"DELAY"`

Hold the packet until you manually release it

### Releasing Delayed Packets[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#releasing-delayed-packets)

#### `onUpdate`[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#onupdate)

Called every tick when there are delayed packets waiting. Use this to decide when to release them.

Each `DelayedPacket` exposes:

Property / Method

Description

`packet`

The original `Packet` object

`time`

Epoch ms timestamp of when it was delayed

`direction()`

`"IN"` or `"OUT"`

`hasAged(ms)`

`true` if the packet has been held for at least `ms` milliseconds

### Flushing Packets[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#flushing-packets)

If you need to release everything at once — for example when the script is disabled — use the flush helpers.

> Always call `flushAll()` in your `script.onDisable` callback to avoid packets being stuck indefinitely.

* * *

### Sending Packets Manually[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#sending-packets-manually)

You can also inject your own packets directly to the server.

See [https://github.com/LoseBypass/breeze-docs/blob/main/docs/examples/page-1.md](https://github.com/LoseBypass/breeze-docs/blob/main/docs/examples/page-1.md)
 for a basic implementation of the spoof manager.

Last updated 3 months ago

*   [Setup](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#setup)
    
*   [Intercepting Packets](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#intercepting-packets)
    
*   [SpoofAction Values](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#spoofaction-values)
    
*   [Releasing Delayed Packets](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#releasing-delayed-packets)
    
*   [Flushing Packets](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#flushing-packets)
    
*   [Sending Packets Manually](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof_manager#sending-packets-manually)
    

Copy

    spoofManager.onPacketReceive((packet) => {
        if (packet instanceof S08PacketPlayerPosLook) {
            return "DELAY"; // hold the teleport packet until we're ready
        }
        return "PROCESS";
    });

Copy

    spoofManager.onUpdate((packets) => {
        for (let packet of packets) {
            if (packet.hasAged(1000)) { // release after 1 second
                spoofManager.release(packet);
            }
        }
    });

Copy

    spoofManager.flushAll();      // release all delayed packets
    spoofManager.flushIncoming(); // release only inbound packets
    spoofManager.flushOutgoing(); // release only outbound packets

Copy

    spoofManager.sendPacket(new C05PacketPlayerLook(90.0, 0.0, true), false);

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/rotation\_manager.md).

# Rotation Manager

The \`rotationManager\` namespace lets your script control the player's look direction (yaw/pitch).

\*\*\*

### Setup

Set your priority during initialization. Higher numbers run first.

\`\`\`js
rotationManager.setPriority(10);
\`\`\`

### Registering Callbacks

#### \*\*onRotate\*\*

This is your main rotation callback — called every tick when your script has priority. Call \`rotationManager.rotate()\` inside here.

\`\`\`js
rotationManager.onRotate(() => {
    rotationManager.rotate(targetYaw, targetPitch, 8.0);
});
\`\`\`

#### onUpdateNoRotate

Called every tick when another module has taken priority over yours. Use this to keep internal state updated even when you're not rotating.

\`\`\`js
rotationManager.onUpdateNoRotate(() => {
    // another module is rotating — idle tick
});
\`\`\`

### Rotating

#### \[\`rotationManager.rotate(yaw, pitch, speed)\`\](/api/functions/rotationmanager.rotate.md)

Smoothly steps toward the target yaw/pitch by at most \`speed\` degrees per tick. Returns \`true\` when the target has been reached.

\`\`\`js
rotationManager.onRotate(() => {
    const done = rotationManager.rotate(90.0, 15.0, 6.0);

    if (done) {
        // we're now looking exactly at the target
    }
});
\`\`\`

### Reading the Current Spoofed Angles

You can read back the current spoofed rotation at any time — useful for calculating aim vectors or checking if you're on target.

\`\`\`js
var yaw   = rotationManager.getSpoofedYaw();
var pitch = rotationManager.getSpoofedPitch();
\`\`\`

### Priority List

These are the default priorities for each module in breeze. Highest priority gets to rotate first.

| Module               | Priority |
| -------------------- | -------- |
| SelfTrap             | 45       |
| BedNuker             | 40       |
| AntiFireball         | 35       |
| Scaffold             | 30       |
| Clutch               | 25       |
| MLG                  | 20       |
| KnockBackManipulator | 15       |
| SilentAim            | 10       |
| AutoRod              | 5        |
| KillAura             | 0        |

# PingSpoof | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/examples/pingspoof.md)
.

Copy

    script.description = "Delays all packets by a set amount of time."
    
    const delay = new IntSetting(script, "Delay", "The amount of ms to delay each packet for", 500, 0, 5000);
    
    spoofManager.onPacketSend(packet => {
        return "DELAY";
    });
    
    spoofManager.onUpdate((packets) => {
        for (let packet of packets) {
            if (packet.hasAged(delay.getValue())) {
                spoofManager.release(packet);
            }
        }
    });

Last updated 3 months ago

.

Copy

    script.description = "Mine blocks at twice the speed!";
    
    const sendAnimation = new BooleanSetting(script, "SwingPacket", "If this is enabled the module will also send the needed swing packet", true);
    
    script.addListener("PreTickEvent", (event) => {
        if (mc.playerController.isHittingBlock() && mc.objectMouseOver.blockPos != null) {
            if (mc.playerController.damageBlock(mc.objectMouseOver.blockPos, mc.objectMouseOver.sideHit) && sendAnimation.getValue()) {
                spoofManager.sendPacket(new C0APacketAnimation());
            }
        }
    });

Last updated 3 months ago

# spoofManager.onUpdate | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate.md)
.

> **onUpdate**(`f`): `void`

Register the callback for updates. This is called whenever a packet is delayed.

You can use this to release packets after a certain amount of time.

### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#parameters)

#### f[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#f)

(`packets`) => `void`

### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#returns)

`void`

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#example)

Copy

    spoofManager.onUpdate(packets => {
        for (let packet of packets) {
            if (packet.hasAged(1000)) { // Release after 1 second
                spoofManager.release(packet);
            }
        }
    });

Last updated 8 months ago

*   [Parameters](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#parameters)
    
*   [Returns](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#returns)
    
*   [Example](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate#example)

.

> **interpolateLastTickPos**(`pos`, `lastPos`): `number`

Interpolates a single coordinate between its previous and current tick value, using the current render partial ticks.

### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolatelasttickpos#parameters)

The coordinate as of this tick.

#### lastPos[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolatelasttickpos#lastpos)

`number`

The coordinate as of the previous tick.

### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolatelasttickpos#returns)

`number`

The coordinate where it should be drawn this frame.

### Remarks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolatelasttickpos#remarks)

Use this when you track positions yourself. For entities prefer interpolateEntityPosition.

# spoofManager.onPacketSend | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend.md)
.

> **onPacketSend**(`f`): `void`

Register the callback for outbound packets.

You should return a valid SpoofAction which can be:

*   PROCESS: Does nothing lets the packet continue just like normal.
    
*   CANCEL: Cancels the packet entirely.
    
*   DELAY: Starts delaying the packet until you decide to release it.
    

### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#parameters)

#### f[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#f)

(`packet`) => [`SpoofAction`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/enum_types/spoofaction)

### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#returns)

`void`

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#example)

Copy

    spoofManager.onPacketSend(packet => {
        if (packet instanceof C0BPacketEntityAction && packet.getAction() === "START_SNEAKING") {
            return "CANCEL"; // Cancel sneaking packets
        }
        return "PROCESS";
    });

Last updated 8 months ago

*   [Parameters](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#parameters)
    
*   [Returns](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#returns)
    
*   [Example](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend#example)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)

> **interpolateEntityBB**(`entity`): [`AxisAlignedBB`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/axisalignedbb)

The bounding box of an entity interpolated to this frame, using the current render partial ticks.

#### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb#parameters)

**entity**

[`Entity`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/entity/entity)

The entity to interpolate.

#### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb#returns)

[`AxisAlignedBB`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/axisalignedbb)

The entity's bounding box this frame, or null when no entity was given.

### Call Signature[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb#call-signature-1)

> **interpolateEntityBB**(`entity`, `partialTicks`): [`AxisAlignedBB`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/axisalignedbb)

The bounding box of an entity interpolated to this frame.

#### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb#parameters-1)

**entity**

[`Entity`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/entity/entity)

The entity to interpolate.

**partialTicks**

`number`

The partial ticks, as given by a render event.

#### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb#returns-1)

[`AxisAlignedBB`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/axisalignedbb)

The entity's bounding box this frame, or null when no entity was given.

#### Remarks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb#remarks)

The box is expanded slightly, so an ESP drawn around it does not clip into the entity's model.

# math.findBestVisiblePoint | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint.md)
.

> **findBestVisiblePoint**(`eyes`, `bb`): [`Vec3`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/vec3)

Finds the closest point on a bounding box that can be seen from the given eye position without a block in the way.

### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#parameters)

#### eyes[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#eyes)

[`Vec3`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/vec3)

The position to look from, usually the local player's eyes.

#### bb[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#bb)

[`AxisAlignedBB`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/axisalignedbb)

The bounding box to look at.

### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#returns)

[`Vec3`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/vec3)

The nearest unobstructed point, or null when every point is blocked.

### Remarks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#remarks)

Points along the vertical center of the box are preferred over its corners. This raytraces blocks, so it is not free, call it once per target per tick rather than per frame.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#example)

Last updated 14 days ago

*   [Parameters](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#parameters)
    
*   [Returns](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#returns)
    
*   [Remarks](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#remarks)
    
*   [Example](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint#example)
    

Copy

    const target = mc.pointedEntity;
    const point = math.findBestVisiblePoint(mc.player.getPositionEyes(), target.getEntityBoundingBox());
    if (point !== null) script.log("visible at " + point.getY());

# hudRenderer.drawSVG | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg.md)
.

### Call Signature[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg#call-signature)

> **drawSVG**(`url`, `x`, `y`, `scale`, `color`): `void`

Draws an SVG from an http/https URL, rasterised at the given scale (1.0 = native SVG size in pixels). The download happens asynchronously; the call is silently skipped until ready. Cached per (url, scale) so each distinct scale is sharp.

#### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg#parameters)

**url**

`string`

An http/https SVG URL.

**x**

`number`

The x position.

**y**

`number`

The y position.

**scale**

`number`

The rasterization scale.

**color**

[`Color`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/breeze/util/color)

The flat tint color.

#### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg#returns)

`void`

### Call Signature[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg#call-signature-1)

> **drawSVG**(`url`, `x`, `y`, `scale`, `color`, `glowAmount`): `void`

Draws an SVG from an http/https URL with a soft glow behind it.

#### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg#parameters-1)

**url**

`string`

An http/https SVG URL.

**x**

`number`

The x position.

**y**

`number`

The y position.

**scale**

`number`

The rasterization scale.

**color**

[`Color`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/breeze/util/color)

The flat tint color.

**glowAmount**

`number`

The glow blur radius in pixels.

#### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawsvg#returns-1)

`void`

# hudRenderer.drawTexture | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawtexture.md)
.

> **drawTexture**(`url`, `x`, `y`, `w`, `h`, `tint`): `void`

Draws an image from an http/https URL with a flat colour tint. The download happens asynchronously; the call is silently skipped until the image is ready. Results are cached after the first successful load.

### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawtexture#parameters)

#### url[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/hudrenderer.drawtexture#url)

# spoofManager.onPacketReceive | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive.md)
.

> **onPacketReceive**(`f`): `void`

Register the callback for inbound packets.

You should return a valid SpoofAction which can be:

*   PROCESS: Does nothing lets the packet continue just like normal.
    
*   CANCEL: Cancels the packet entirely.
    
*   DELAY: Starts delaying the packet until you decide to release it.
    

### Parameters[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#parameters)

#### f[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#f)

(`packet`) => [`SpoofAction`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/enum_types/spoofaction)

### Returns[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#returns)

`void`

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#example)

Copy

    spoofManager.onPacketReceive(packet => {
        if (packet instanceof S08PacketPlayerPosLook) {
            return "DELAY"; // Delay the packet for now until we release it.
        }
        return "PROCESS";
    });

Last updated 8 months ago

*   [Parameters](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#parameters)
    
*   [Returns](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#returns)
    
*   [Example](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive#example)

.

A simple boolean setting.

### Remarks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/breeze/settings/booleansetting#remarks)

Can only be created while initializing the script.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/breeze/settings/booleansetting#example)

Copy

    const alwaysAttack = new BooleanSetting("AlwaysAttack", "If the module will always attack regardles of player health.", false);

# HUD Scripting | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting.md)
.

HUD scripts let you render custom 2D overlays on screen using the `hudManager` and `hudRenderer` namespaces. Elements are draggable in the HUD editor and their positions persist to config automatically.

* * *

Render Pipeline[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#render-pipeline)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Each frame breeze renders scripted HUD elements in two passes:

1.  **Background pass** — your `onBackground` callback returns a list of `RoundedRect` shapes. These are collected alongside all other HUD backgrounds and rendered together (with blur/shadow) before any content is drawn.
    
2.  **Render pass** — your `onRender` callback runs. Use `hudRenderer` functions here to draw text, shapes, and items.
    

All drawing in both passes uses screen-space coordinates with the origin at the top-left corner (x right, y down). The `hudManager.x()` / `hudManager.y()` values give you the top-left corner of your element's current dragged position — offset everything you draw from those values.

* * *

hudManager[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#hudmanager)

--------------------------------------------------------------------

Registers callbacks and exposes layout information for your element.

### Callbacks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#callbacks)

All callbacks must be registered during script initialization.

Copy

    // Called every frame to draw your element's content.
    // x, y, scale are passed in directly so you don't need to call hudManager.x() etc. inside the callback.
    hudManager.onRender((x, y, scale) => {
        // use hudRenderer here
    });
    
    // Returns an array of RoundedRect shapes drawn as the blurred background.
    hudManager.onBackground(() => {
        return [new RoundedRect(hudManager.x(), hudManager.y(), width, height, 4 * hudManager.scale())];
    });
    
    // Returns the pixel width of your element. Used by the HUD editor for snapping.
    hudManager.onWidth(() => 160);
    
    // Returns the pixel height of your element.
    hudManager.onHeight(() => 40);

### Position & Layout[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#position-and-layout)

Function

Returns

Description

`hudManager.x()`

`number`

Left edge of the element on screen

`hudManager.y()`

`number`

Top edge of the element on screen

`hudManager.scale()`

`number`

User-configured HUD scale (use this to scale padding, radii, and sizes)

`hudManager.fontHeight()`

`number`

Pixel height of the selected HUD font

`hudManager.x()`, `hudManager.y()`, and `hudManager.scale()` are also passed directly into `onRender` as `(x, y, scale)` — you only need to call these getters explicitly in `onBackground`, `onWidth`, or `onHeight`.

* * *

hudRenderer[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#hudrenderer)

----------------------------------------------------------------------

All drawing functions take absolute screen coordinates. Inside `onRender` use the `x` and `y` parameters passed to you; in other contexts call `hudManager.x()` / `hudManager.y()`.

### Text[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#text)

### Rectangles[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#rectangles)

### Circles[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#circles)

### Items[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#items)

* * *

RoundedRect[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#roundedrect)

----------------------------------------------------------------------

Used exclusively in `onBackground` to describe the blurred background region.

* * *

Minimal Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#minimal-example)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------

* * *

Tips[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#tips)

--------------------------------------------------------

*   **Scale everything** — multiply all padding, corner radii, and icon sizes by `hudManager.scale()` so the element looks right at any user scale.
    
*   **Guard nulls** — `mc.player` and `mc.world` can be null (e.g. on the main menu). Check before accessing them.
    
*   **Background vs content** — only use `onBackground` for the blurred rect outline. Decorations and text go in `onRender`.
    
*   **Dynamic size** — `onWidth` / `onHeight` are called every frame, so you can return a value based on current content.
    

* * *

See Also[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#see-also)

----------------------------------------------------------------

*   [hudManager namespace](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/hudmanager)
    
*   [hudRenderer namespace](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/hudrenderer)
    
*   [Defining Settings](https://scripting.breeze.rip/defining_settings)
    
*   [Example: PlayerInfo](https://scripting.breeze.rip/examples/playerinfo)
    

Last updated 24 days ago

*   [Render Pipeline](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#render-pipeline)
    
*   [hudManager](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#hudmanager)
    
*   [Callbacks](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#callbacks)
    
*   [Position & Layout](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#position-and-layout)
    
*   [hudRenderer](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#hudrenderer)
    
*   [Text](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#text)
    
*   [Rectangles](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#rectangles)
    
*   [Circles](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#circles)
    
*   [Items](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#items)
    
*   [RoundedRect](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#roundedrect)
    
*   [Minimal Example](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#minimal-example)
    
*   [Tips](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#tips)
    
*   [See Also](https://scripting.breeze.rip/hud_scripting#see-also)
    

Copy

    // Pixel width of a string in the current HUD font.
    const w = hudRenderer.stringWidth("Hello");
    
    // Draw text. Optional fifth argument enables a drop shadow.
    hudRenderer.drawString(x, y, "Hello", new Color(255, 255, 255, 255));
    hudRenderer.drawString(x, y, "Hello", new Color(255, 255, 255, 255), true);

Copy

    // Solid color, uniform corner radius.
    hudRenderer.drawRect(x, y, w, h, radius, color);
    
    // Solid color, per-corner radii (TL, TR, BR, BL).
    hudRenderer.drawRect(x, y, w, h, rtl, rtr, rbr, rbl, color);
    
    // Two-color gradient. vertical=true → top-to-bottom, false → left-to-right.
    hudRenderer.drawRectGradient(x, y, w, h, radius, color1, color2, vertical);
    
    // Solid color with an outer glow. glowColor alpha controls max intensity.
    hudRenderer.drawRectGlow(x, y, w, h, radius, color, glowRadius, glowColor);
    
    // Uses the current HUD effect (rainbow / gradient / pulse / none). alpha is 0.0–1.0.
    hudRenderer.drawRectWithEffect(x, y, w, h, radius, alpha);

Copy

    hudRenderer.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, color);
    
    // Hollow ring.
    hudRenderer.drawRing(cx, cy, radius, thickness, color);
    
    // Uses the current HUD effect. alpha is 0.0–1.0.
    hudRenderer.drawCircleWithEffect(cx, cy, radius, alpha);

Copy

    // Draw an ItemStack icon. size=16 is native resolution.
    hudRenderer.drawItemStack(stack, x, y, size);
    
    // With count and durability bar overlay.
    hudRenderer.drawItemStack(stack, x, y, size, true);

Copy

    // Uniform radius
    new RoundedRect(x, y, w, h, radius)
    
    // Per-corner radii (TL, TR, BR, BL)
    new RoundedRect(x, y, w, h, rtl, rtr, rbr, rbl)

Copy

    script.description = "Displays the player's health.";
    
    const PAD = 6;
    
    hudManager.onWidth(() => 120);
    hudManager.onHeight(() => hudManager.fontHeight() + PAD * 2 * hudManager.scale());
    
    hudManager.onBackground(() => {
        const s = hudManager.scale();
        return [new RoundedRect(hudManager.x(), hudManager.y(), 120, hudManager.fontHeight() + PAD * 2 * s, 4 * s)];
    });
    
    hudManager.onRender((x, y, scale) => {
        const pad = PAD * scale;
    
        const hp = mc.player != null ? Math.ceil(mc.player.getHealth()) : 0;
        hudRenderer.drawString(x + pad, y + pad, "Health: " + hp, new Color(255, 255, 255, 220), true);
    });

---

# PlayerInfo | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/examples/playerinfo.md)
.

Demonstrates `hudManager`, `hudRenderer`, `RoundedRect`, `drawRectGradient`, `drawItemStack`, and user-configurable settings.

Copy

    script.description = "Shows health, armor, coordinates, and held item on the HUD.";
    
    // ── Settings ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const showCoords    = new BooleanSetting(script, "Coordinates", "Show XYZ position.", true);
    const showArmor     = new BooleanSetting(script, "Armor", "Show armor value.", true);
    const showItem      = new BooleanSetting(script, "Held Item", "Show held item icon.", true);
    const healthColor   = new ColorSetting(script, "Health Color", "Color of the health bar.", new Color(200, 60, 60, 220));
    const textColor     = new ColorSetting(script, "Text Color", "Color of all text labels.", new Color(255, 255, 255, 220));
    
    // ── Layout constants (scaled in render) ───────────────────────────────────────
    const BASE_W        = 160;
    const PAD           = 5;
    const BAR_H         = 4;
    const ITEM_SIZE     = 16;
    
    // ── Dynamic dimensions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function elementHeight() {
        const s   = hudManager.scale();
        const fh  = hudManager.fontHeight();
        let rows  = 1;                            // health bar row
        if (showArmor.getValue())  rows++;
        if (showCoords.getValue()) rows++;
        const itemRow = showItem.getValue() ? ITEM_SIZE * s + PAD * s : 0;
        return PAD * s + rows * (fh + PAD * s) + BAR_H * s + PAD * s + itemRow;
    }
    
    hudManager.onWidth(()  => BASE_W * hudManager.scale());
    hudManager.onHeight(() => elementHeight());
    
    // ── Background ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    hudManager.onBackground(() => {
        const s = hudManager.scale();
        const r = 4 * s;
        return [new RoundedRect(hudManager.x(), hudManager.y(), BASE_W * s, elementHeight(), r)];
    });
    
    // ── Render ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    hudManager.onRender((x, y, scale) => {
        if (mc.player == null) return;
    
        const s   = scale;
        const fh  = hudManager.fontHeight();
        const pad = PAD * s;
        const w   = BASE_W * s;
        const r   = 3 * s;
    
        let cursor = y + pad;
    
        // Health label + bar
        const hp    = mc.player.getHealth();
        const maxHp = mc.player.getMaxHealth();
        const hpPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, hp / maxHp));
        const barW  = w - pad * 2;
        const barH  = BAR_H * s;
    
        hudRenderer.drawString(x + pad, cursor, "Health  " + Math.ceil(hp) + " / " + maxHp, textColor.getValue(), true);
        cursor += fh + pad * 0.5;
    
        // track background
        hudRenderer.drawRect(x + pad, cursor, barW, barH, r, new Color(40, 40, 40, 180));
        // filled portion: green → red gradient based on health
        const barFill = barW * hpPct;
        if (barFill > 0) {
            const c = healthColor.getValue();
            const bright = new Color(Math.min(255, c.red() + 60), Math.min(255, c.green() + 60), c.blue(), c.alpha != null ? c.alpha() : 220);
            hudRenderer.drawRectGradient(x + pad, cursor, barFill, barH, r, bright, c, false);
        }
        cursor += barH + pad;
    
        // Armor
        if (showArmor.getValue()) {
            const armor = mc.player.inventory != null
                ? (mc.player.inventory.armorInventory != null
                    ? mc.player.inventory.armorInventory.length
                    : 0)
                : 0;
            // armor is 0-20; read total armor via a simple formula
            hudRenderer.drawString(x + pad, cursor, "Armor   " + Math.round(mc.player.getHealth()), textColor.getValue(), true);
            // Note: use mc.player for a real armor value — this is illustrative
            cursor += fh + pad;
        }
    
        // Coordinates
        if (showCoords.getValue()) {
            const px = Math.floor(mc.player.posX);
            const py = Math.floor(mc.player.posY);
            const pz = Math.floor(mc.player.posZ);
            hudRenderer.drawString(x + pad, cursor, px + "  " + py + "  " + pz, textColor.getValue(), true);
            cursor += fh + pad;
        }
    
        // Held item
        if (showItem.getValue()) {
            const stack = mc.player.getHeldItem != null ? mc.player.getHeldItem() : null;
            if (stack != null && stack.exists()) {
                const iconSize = ITEM_SIZE * s;
                hudRenderer.drawItemStack(stack, x + pad, cursor, iconSize, true);
                const name = stack.getDisplayName();
                hudRenderer.drawString(x + pad + iconSize + pad, cursor + (iconSize - fh) / 2, name, textColor.getValue(), true);
            }
        }
    });

Last updated 24 days ago

---

# RaytraceResult | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult.md)
.

Represents the result of a raytrace.

### Properties[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#properties)

#### blockPos[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#blockpos)

> `readonly` **blockPos**: [`BlockPos`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/blockpos)

The block position the raytrace might have hit.

**Remarks**

May be null if the raytrace didn't hit a block.

* * *

#### entityHit[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#entityhit)

> `readonly` **entityHit**: [`Entity`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/entity/entity)

The entity the raytrace might have hit.

**Remarks**

May be null if the raytrace didn't hit an entity.

* * *

#### hitVec[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#hitvec)

> `readonly` **hitVec**: [`Vec3`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/vec3)

The hit vector of the raytrace, can be null.

* * *

#### sideHit[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#sidehit)

> `readonly` **sideHit**: `string`

The side of the block the raytrace might have hit. Will be a EnumFacing.

**Remarks**

May be null if the raytrace didn't hit a block.

### Methods[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#methods)

#### getHitType()[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/raytraceresult#gethittype)

> **getHitType**(): [`HitType`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/enum_types/hittype)

**Returns**

[`HitType`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/enum_types/hittype)

The type of hit that was made. One of: BLOCK, ENTITY, MISS.

# math | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/math.md)
.

The math namespace, the same helpers breeze uses internally for tick interpolation, clamping and visibility checks.

### Remarks[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/math#remarks)

Entity positions only move once per tick. Anything you draw in a render event should use the interpolated variants here, otherwise it will visibly stutter.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/math#example)

Copy

    script.addListener("Render3DEvent", (event) => {
      if (mc.pointedEntity === null) return;
      const box = math.interpolateEntityBB(mc.pointedEntity, event.getPartialTicks());
      renderer.boundingESPBox(box, new Color(255, 0, 0), 2, false);
    });

### Functions[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/math#functions)

*   [clamp](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.clamp)
    
*   [findBestVisiblePoint](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.findbestvisiblepoint)
    
*   [getCenter](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.getcenter)
    
*   [interpolateEntityBB](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentitybb)
    
*   [interpolateEntityPosition](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolateentityposition)
    
*   [interpolateLastTickPos](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/math.interpolatelasttickpos)
    

Last updated 14 days ago

# rotationManager | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/rotationmanager.md)
.

The rotation manager.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/rotationmanager#example)

Copy

    rotationManager.setPriority(10);
    
    rotationManager.onRotate((updateNoRotate) => {
        // Aim 45° yaw, 10° pitch at 6°/tick
        rotationManager.rotate(45.0, 10.0, 6.0);
    });
    
    rotationManager.onUpdateNoRotate(() => {
        // Called when this module has lost priority to another rotation module
    });

### Functions[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/rotationmanager#functions)

*   [getSpoofedPitch](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/rotationmanager.getspoofedpitch)

*   [onRotate](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/rotationmanager.onrotate)

*   [rotate](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/rotationmanager.rotate)
    
*   [setPriority](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/rotationmanager.setpriority)

# PreAttackEvent | Scripting

. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/preattackevent.md)
.

KillAura calls this before attacking, you can use this to script a custom autoblock.

### Properties[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/preattackevent#properties)

#### attack[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/preattackevent#attack)

> **attack**: `boolean`

If the KillAura will attack this tick, can also be set to true if you want to force an attack.

**Remarks**

The attack will only happen if hitVec is not null.

* * *

#### hitVec[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/preattackevent#hitvec)

> `readonly` **hitVec**: [`Vec3`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/util/vec3)

The position vector for where we hit the enemy.

* * *

#### swing[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/preattackevent#swing)

> **swing**: `boolean`

Determines if the swing animation will occur this tick, can be set to modify swing behavior.

# SpoofInputEvent | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/spoofinputevent.md)
.

Can be used to spoof keys being held down or released.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/spoofinputevent#example)

Copy

    script.addListener("SpoofInputEvent", (event) => {
        if (event.keyBinding == mc.gameSettings.keyBindSprint) {
            event.pressed = true;
        }
    });

### Properties[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/spoofinputevent#properties)

#### pressed[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/spoofinputevent#pressed)

> **pressed**: `boolean`

Modify this to spoof the key state.

### Methods[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/spoofinputevent#methods)

#### getKeyBinding()[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/spoofinputevent#getkeybinding)

> **getKeyBinding**(): [`MCKeyBinding`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/client/settings/mckeybinding)

Gets the key binding that was pressed.

**Returns**

[`MCKeyBinding`](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/client/settings/mckeybinding)

Last updated 8 months ago

# spoofManager | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/spoofmanager.md)
.

Spoofing manager.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/spoofmanager#example)

Copy

    const delay = new IntSetting(script, "Delay", "The amount of ms to delay each packet for", 500, 0, 5000);
    
    spoofManager.onPacketSend(packet => {
        return "DELAY";
    });
    
    spoofManager.onUpdate((packets) => {
        for (let packet of packets) {
            if (packet.hasAged(delay.getValue())) {
                spoofManager.release(packet);
            }
        }
    });

### Functions[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/namespaces/spoofmanager#functions)

*   [flushAll](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.flushall)
    
*   [flushIncoming](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.flushincoming)
    
*   [flushOutgoing](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.flushoutgoing)
    
*   [onPacketReceive](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketreceive)
    
*   [onPacketSend](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onpacketsend)
    
*   [onUpdate](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.onupdate)
    
*   [release](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.release)
    
*   [sendPacket](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.sendpacket)
    
*   [setPriority](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/functions/spoofmanager.setpriority)
    

Last updated 8 months ago

# MotionEvent | Scripting

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/motionevent.md)
.

Gets called when the player's motion is updated, you can use this event to override the movement.

### Example[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/motionevent#example)

Copy

    script.addListener("MotionEvent", (event) => {
        // make the player 50% faster    event.x *= 1.5;
        event.z *= 1.5;
    });

### Extends[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/events/motionevent#extends)

#### windowClick()[](https://scripting.breeze.rip/api/net/minecraft/client/playercontrollermp#windowclick)

> **windowClick**(`windowId`, `slot`, `button`, `mode`): `object`

Perform an inventory click (client-side send + local container logic).

Parameters:

*   windowId: The container window ID (0 = player inventory, other IDs for open GUIs).
    
*   slot: Slot index to click. Use -999 to click outside (cursor drop).
    
*   button: Mouse button / index depending on mode.
    
*   mode: Integer 0..6: 0=PICKUP, 1=QUICK\_MOVE, 2=SWAP, 3=CLONE, 4=THROW, 5=QUICK\_CRAFT, 6=PICKUP\_ALL
    

Returns: The ItemStack on the cursor after the click (or null if empty).

**Parameters**

**windowId**

`number`

windowId

**slot**

`number`

slot

**button**

`number`

button

**mode**

`number`

mode

**Returns**

`object`

ItemStack | null

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://scripting.breeze.rip/spoof\_manager.md).

# Spoof Manager

The \`spoofManager\` namespace lets your script intercept, cancel, delay, and release packets in both directions. Like the rotation manager, it uses a priority system for when multiple scripts are active.

This manager is mainly meant for making lag based modules. For basic packet manipulation we recommend \[PacketSendEvent\](/api/events/packetsendevent.md).

\*\*\*

### Setup

Set your priority during initialization.

\`\`\`javascript
spoofManager.setPriority(10);
\`\`\`

\*\*\*

### Intercepting Packets

#### \`onPacketSend\`

Called for every outbound packet (client → server). Return a \`SpoofAction\` to decide what happens to it.

\`\`\`javascript
spoofManager.onPacketSend((packet) => {
    if (packet instanceof C0BPacketEntityAction) {
        if (packet.getAction() === "START\_SNEAKING") {
            return "CANCEL"; // block sneak packets from reaching the server
        }
    }
    return "PROCESS"; // let everything else through
});
\`\`\`

\*\*\*

#### \`onPacketReceive\`

Called for every inbound packet (server → client). Same \`SpoofAction\` return values apply.

\`\`\`javascript
spoofManager.onPacketReceive((packet) => {
    if (packet instanceof S08PacketPlayerPosLook) {
        return "DELAY"; // hold the teleport packet until we're ready
    }
    return "PROCESS";
});
\`\`\`

\*\*\*

### SpoofAction Values

| Value       | Description                                           |
| ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| \`"PROCESS"\` | Do nothing, let the packet continue as normal         |
| \`"CANCEL"\`  | Drop the packet entirely — it will never be processed |
| \`"DELAY"\`   | Hold the packet until you manually release it         |

### Releasing Delayed Packets

#### \`onUpdate\`

Called every tick when there are delayed packets waiting. Use this to decide when to release them.

\`\`\`javascript
spoofManager.onUpdate((packets) => {
    for (let packet of packets) {
        if (packet.hasAged(1000)) { // release after 1 second
            spoofManager.release(packet);
        }
    }
});
\`\`\`

Each \`DelayedPacket\` exposes:

| Property / Method | Description                                                       |
| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`packet\`          | The original \`Packet\` object                                      |
| \`time\`            | Epoch ms timestamp of when it was delayed                         |
| \`direction()\`     | \`"IN"\` or \`"OUT"\`                                                 |
| \`hasAged(ms)\`     | \`true\` if the packet has been held for at least \`ms\` milliseconds |

### Flushing Packets

If you need to release everything at once — for example when the script is disabled — use the flush helpers.

\`\`\`typescript
spoofManager.flushAll();      // release all delayed packets
spoofManager.flushIncoming(); // release only inbound packets
spoofManager.flushOutgoing(); // release only outbound packets
\`\`\`

> Always call \`flushAll()\` in your \`script.onDisable\` callback to avoid packets being stuck indefinitely.

\*\*\*

### Sending Packets Manually

You can also inject your own packets directly to the server.

\`\`\`typescript
spoofManager.sendPacket(new C05PacketPlayerLook(90.0, 0.0, true), false);
\`\`\`

{% hint style="info" %}
See \[https://github.com/LoseBypass/breeze-docs/blob/main/docs/examples/page-1.md\](https://github.com/LoseBypass/breeze-docs/blob/main/docs/examples/page-1.md "mention") for a basic implementation of the spoof manager.
{% endhint %}

# HUD Scripting

HUD scripts let you render custom 2D overlays on screen using the \`hudManager\` and \`hudRenderer\` namespaces. Elements are draggable in the HUD editor and their positions persist to config automatically.

\*\*\*

## Render Pipeline

Each frame breeze renders scripted HUD elements in two passes:

1. \*\*Background pass\*\* — your \`onBackground\` callback returns a list of \`RoundedRect\` shapes. These are collected alongside all other HUD backgrounds and rendered together (with blur/shadow) before any content is drawn.
2. \*\*Render pass\*\* — your \`onRender\` callback runs. Use \`hudRenderer\` functions here to draw text, shapes, and items.

All drawing in both passes uses screen-space coordinates with the origin at the top-left corner (x right, y down). The \`hudManager.x()\` / \`hudManager.y()\` values give you the top-left corner of your element's current dragged position — offset everything you draw from those values.

\*\*\*

## hudManager

Registers callbacks and exposes layout information for your element.

### Callbacks

All callbacks must be registered during script initialization.

\`\`\`js
// Called every frame to draw your element's content.
// x, y, scale are passed in directly so you don't need to call hudManager.x() etc. inside the callback.
hudManager.onRender((x, y, scale) => {
    // use hudRenderer here
});

// Returns an array of RoundedRect shapes drawn as the blurred background.
hudManager.onBackground(() => {
    return \[new RoundedRect(hudManager.x(), hudManager.y(), width, height, 4 \* hudManager.scale())\];
});

// Returns the pixel width of your element. Used by the HUD editor for snapping.
hudManager.onWidth(() => 160);

// Returns the pixel height of your element.
hudManager.onHeight(() => 40);
\`\`\`

### Position & Layout

| Function                  | Returns  | Description                                                             |
| ------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`hudManager.x()\`          | \`number\` | Left edge of the element on screen                                      |
| \`hudManager.y()\`          | \`number\` | Top edge of the element on screen                                       |
| \`hudManager.scale()\`      | \`number\` | User-configured HUD scale (use this to scale padding, radii, and sizes) |
| \`hudManager.fontHeight()\` | \`number\` | Pixel height of the selected HUD font                                   |

\`hudManager.x()\`, \`hudManager.y()\`, and \`hudManager.scale()\` are also passed directly into \`onRender\` as \`(x, y, scale)\` — you only need to call these getters explicitly in \`onBackground\`, \`onWidth\`, or \`onHeight\`.

\*\*\*

## hudRenderer

All drawing functions take absolute screen coordinates. Inside \`onRender\` use the \`x\` and \`y\` parameters passed to you; in other contexts call \`hudManager.x()\` / \`hudManager.y()\`.

### Text

\`\`\`js
// Pixel width of a string in the current HUD font.
const w = hudRenderer.stringWidth("Hello");

// Draw text. Optional fifth argument enables a drop shadow.
hudRenderer.drawString(x, y, "Hello", new Color(255, 255, 255, 255));
hudRenderer.drawString(x, y, "Hello", new Color(255, 255, 255, 255), true);
\`\`\`

### Rectangles

\`\`\`js
// Solid color, uniform corner radius.
hudRenderer.drawRect(x, y, w, h, radius, color);

// Solid color, per-corner radii (TL, TR, BR, BL).
hudRenderer.drawRect(x, y, w, h, rtl, rtr, rbr, rbl, color);

// Two-color gradient. vertical=true → top-to-bottom, false → left-to-right.
hudRenderer.drawRectGradient(x, y, w, h, radius, color1, color2, vertical);

// Solid color with an outer glow. glowColor alpha controls max intensity.
hudRenderer.drawRectGlow(x, y, w, h, radius, color, glowRadius, glowColor);

// Uses the current HUD effect (rainbow / gradient / pulse / none). alpha is 0.0–1.0.
hudRenderer.drawRectWithEffect(x, y, w, h, radius, alpha);
\`\`\`

### Circles

\`\`\`js
hudRenderer.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, color);

// Hollow ring.
hudRenderer.drawRing(cx, cy, radius, thickness, color);

// Uses the current HUD effect. alpha is 0.0–1.0.
hudRenderer.drawCircleWithEffect(cx, cy, radius, alpha);
\`\`\`

### Items

\`\`\`js
// Draw an ItemStack icon. size=16 is native resolution.
hudRenderer.drawItemStack(stack, x, y, size);

// With count and durability bar overlay.
hudRenderer.drawItemStack(stack, x, y, size, true);
\`\`\`

\*\*\*

## RoundedRect

Used exclusively in \`onBackground\` to describe the blurred background region.

\`\`\`js
// Uniform radius
new RoundedRect(x, y, w, h, radius)

// Per-corner radii (TL, TR, BR, BL)
new RoundedRect(x, y, w, h, rtl, rtr, rbr, rbl)
\`\`\`

\*\*\*

## Minimal Example

\`\`\`js
script.description = "Displays the player's health.";

const PAD = 6;

hudManager.onWidth(() => 120);
hudManager.onHeight(() => hudManager.fontHeight() + PAD \* 2 \* hudManager.scale());

hudManager.onBackground(() => {
    const s = hudManager.scale();
    return \[new RoundedRect(hudManager.x(), hudManager.y(), 120, hudManager.fontHeight() + PAD \* 2 \* s, 4 \* s)\];
});

hudManager.onRender((x, y, scale) => {
    const pad = PAD \* scale;

    const hp = mc.player != null ? Math.ceil(mc.player.getHealth()) : 0;
    hudRenderer.drawString(x + pad, y + pad, "Health: " + hp, new Color(255, 255, 255, 220), true);
});
\`\`\`

\*\*\*

## Tips

\* \*\*Scale everything\*\* — multiply all padding, corner radii, and icon sizes by \`hudManager.scale()\` so the element looks right at any user scale.
\* \*\*Guard nulls\*\* — \`mc.player\` and \`mc.world\` can be null (e.g. on the main menu). Check before accessing them.
\* \*\*Background vs content\*\* — only use \`onBackground\` for the blurred rect outline. Decorations and text go in \`onRender\`.
\* \*\*Dynamic size\*\* — \`onWidth\` / \`onHeight\` are called every frame, so you can return a value based on current content.

\*\*\*

\* \[hudRenderer namespace\](/api/namespaces/hudrenderer.md)
